## `AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Thermal-Shock Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Clay Brick Compressive Strength & Efflorescence Lime Popping
- **Intro:** Thermal-Shock Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Clay Brick Compressive Strength & Efflorescence Lime Popping — Pre-Start Safety and Equipment Verification: Thermocouple accuracy: ±1.5°C. Load cell accuracy: ±0.5% of reading. Data acquisition sampling rate: 1 Hz ±0.1 Hz.
- **Key tolerance:** Thermocouple accuracy: ±1.5°C. Load cell accuracy: ±0.5% of reading. Data acquisition sampling rate: 1 Hz ±0.1 Hz.
- **Standards:** `ISO 7500-1:2018`, `ASTM C67-20 Standard Test Methods for Sampling and Testing Brick`, `ASTM C216-22 Standard Specification for Facing Brick (FBS, FBX, `, `ISO 6782:1982`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-2392-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
